## Preserved J1531 adaptive-loss preregistration: no real outputs then

The [actual schema3registration](evidence/expert_switching_loss_20261107_registration_v1.json)
keeps the original six complete experts and uniform-identity/Jeffreys
Beta-half-half switch prior. It uses their original saved binary64 bounded
NET-loss labels rather than natural source-wealth relatives. No hazard
prior, temperature, window or gain is selected from later portfolio outcomes.

The new adaptive source Module records a prior-rate next-identity forecast,
source played loss, mix loss and nonnegative gap for each matured source
month. The rate is log-six divided by **past** cumulative mixability gap.
Only after the label matures is the gap updated and the new source history
refiltered at its new current rate. Keeping an old-rate posterior would be a
different rule. Per-month common-loss centering is an analytic invariant;
the original labels are unchanged.

Exactly equal labels and zero-gap infinite-rate cases use their analytic
identities. There is no negative-gap clipping, epsilon floor or repair of
an old learner. No static finite-source AdaHedge reference bound is asserted
for this different expert-sequence class. Source gaps, rates, partitions
and posteriors are not main NET wealth, calibrated market probability,
physical turnover or funded DD protection.

by60Chebyshev hazard nodes at the independently updated current rate.
Both methods use only past original source labels and correctly weight hazard
nodes by evidence. The degree119predictive numerator is covered.
Cached complete-policy and memory Adapters use the same opinion Interface.
No old source learner, gap model, target generator, shadow, account or
diagnostic program is executed.

## Preserved read-only J1490 confidence scope: different comparator, mostly active loss months

The [complete saved-record diagnosis](evidence/confidence_regret_scope_20261106_v1.json)
uses the same saved played-loss sequence. It does not execute a second learner,
replace confidence ratings, generate targets or replay any account.
The [frozen method](CONFIDENCE_REGRET_SCOPE_V1.md) decomposes plain excess into
confidence-weighted excess plus omitted-participation excess using exact
Fractions of saved decimal and binary64 operands.

| Complete source policy | Plain source-game regret | Confidence-weighted regret | Omitted-participation excess |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Profit-share confirmation | -1.631499 | -1.631499 | 0.000000 |
| Diversified defense | -19.190805 | -19.190805 | 0.000000 |
| Conditional covariance hedge | -3.775618 | -3.775618 | 0.000000 |
| Month-end commitment | 4.861426 | 4.861426 | 0.000000 |
| Benchmark opportunity | -12.346862 | -12.346862 | 0.000000 |
| Wealth-feedback protection | -14.300466 | -2.917521 | -11.382945 |

Positive regret means the learner's source-game loss exceeds the expert's;
negative means less. For the specialist, confidence weighting makes that
negative learner advantage smaller in magnitude. It is not automatically
an improvement in financial performance. The source-game comparator changes
which periods receive weight, not what the actual played loss was.
There is no always-active alternative account or new unweighted learner.

The other five policies have confidence exactly one in all 119 complete
months, so their two comparator values coincide. The specialist has nine
exact-zero and 110 partial prior-origin ratings, never a full rating.
Their sum is 30.6508561114, not an independent sample count, time-in-market
claim or calibrated probability. Exact whole-history comparator identities
hold; differences from saved rounded cumulative regret are at most
5.46e-159, within the original 1e-12 coherence limit. No old state is repaired.

| Original main path | Complete source months | Main loss months | Partial rating at origin in loss months | Zero rating at origin in loss months | Rating increased by month end in loss months |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 10y-base | 119 | 35 | 32 | 3 | 29 |
| 10y-stress | 119 | 39 | 36 | 3 | 31 |
| 5y-base | 59 | 18 | 17 | 1 | 15 |
| 5y-stress | 59 | 20 | 19 | 1 | 16 |

Thus most loss months do not begin with a completely sleeping specialist.
In every zero-origin loss case above, its rating becomes positive by month end.
That later value was not available at the prior origin and cannot be backdated
into the month's decision. An increase after loss is a retrospective
association, not causal evidence that earlier or larger participation would
have prevented loss. The four paths and their overlapping horizons are not
independent observations; no forecast calibration or new trading rule follows.

## Historical read-only J1416 return-cost bridge

The [complete read-only V2accounting](evidence/quintet_return_cost_bridge_20261106_v2.json)
pairs all16already completed opportunity/conditional-volatility main
books. It preserves8pairs,30128original return rows,32input/resolved
records and80exact binary64primitive/scalar prefixes. No source solver,
shadow,selector,target,main account,BT or financial trial is rerun.

Each net log component is`log(recorded_NAV/previous_recorded_NAV)`.
The cost term is`log1p(recorded_cost/recorded_NAV)`.
Their sum is the recorded-path gross log component,independently checked
against`log((NAV+cost)/previousNAV)`. Paired differences satisfy
**net-log gap = recorded-path gross-log gap - cost-log-drag difference**.
The components have natural-log relative-growth units,not annual CAGR.

| Paired law | Path | Net-log gap | Recorded-path gross-log gap | Cost-log-drag difference | Paid-cost difference USD |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Equal fifths | 10y-base | -0.25306947 | -0.23509413 | 0.01797534 | 110.04 |
| Equal fifths | 10y-stress | -0.25853250 | -0.23483761 | 0.02369488 | 107.36 |
| Equal fifths | 5y-base | -0.10764311 | -0.10700365 | 0.00063947 | -16.80 |
| Equal fifths | 5y-stress | -0.10983293 | -0.10540366 | 0.00442928 | 19.43 |
| Rolling pairs | 10y-base | -0.32274672 | -0.31199610 | 0.01075063 | 6.35 |
| Rolling pairs | 10y-stress | -0.28054193 | -0.26855710 | 0.01198482 | -158.61 |
| Rolling pairs | 5y-base | -0.16282123 | -0.16321025 | -0.00038902 | 10.91 |
| Rolling pairs | 5y-stress | -0.12462203 | -0.13044576 | -0.00582373 | -19.96 |

Every paired net-log gap and remaining realized-path gross-log gap
is negative. Cost-log drag worsens six gaps and offsets part of two.
Equal5y-base and rolling10y-stress have lower paid-dollar costs but
larger log-cost drag;rolling5y-base has higher dollar costs but lower
log-cost drag. Costs are scaled by the actual capital path and timing,
not simply dollars per policy. The fee component does not exhaust
the observed growth loss.

The gross component still contains actual net-financed holdings and
fills. It is not a fee-free funded-policy replay,isolated causal fee
effect or expert-alpha attribution. These overlapping cases are not
independent samples or forward proof. No old CAGR,Sharpe,drawdown,
benchmark gate or source admission is recomputed or revised.

before the first bridge row,334mismatches on the first10ybook.

the original`research_program.review_market`delivery,which matches
all16recorded RF columns exactly on every2512/1254original date.
No RF view,source Adapter,missing-date filling or tolerance waiver
is added;Term and all other blocked-source gates remain closed.

checkpoint718,both recorded before any V2bridge output.

## Signed score normalizer: frequent negative numerators, no causal DD attribution

The [complete V2 read-only survey](evidence/forecast_risk_normalizer_diagnosis_20261106_v2.json)
consumes only the preserved allocation audit underlying both completed forecast
portfolios. It does not rerun their score kernel, ridge fit, target builder,
main account, benchmark or historical verifier. It differs from the earlier
future-variance/ERC calibration diagnosis: this is an identity of the
**already recorded** post-cost forecast numerator and risk normalizer.

| Recorded forecaster | Scored / unscored origins | Selected positive / negative / zero numerator | All eligible numerators negative, origins | All-pair positive / negative / zero numerator | Python-round differences: all / selected keys |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Unconditional unit ridge | 114 / 7 | 22 / 92 / 0 | 92 | 22 / 3170 / 0 | 0 / 0 |
| Contextual unit ridge | 114 / 7 | 34 / 80 / 0 | 80 | 59 / 3133 / 0 | 1 / 0 |

Each model retains all121origins and3192evaluable pair rows, with no source
unscorable pair dropped. All6384source keys match the actual scalar protocol
bit-for-bit. No selected negative numerator occurs when a nonnegative menu
alternative exists. Selected binding risk windows are48training/66validation
for unconditional and49/65for contextual; there are no equal-risk ties here.
These overlapping pair/model counts are not independent economic trials.

For the smaller recorded post-cost SPY/QQQ numerator`u`, the minimum of four
scores is`sqrt252*u/max(risks)`when positive and
`sqrt252*u/min(risks)`when negative. Thus at fixed negative numerator a larger
risk estimate increases the continuous unrounded score; a positive numerator
has the opposite derivative. At zero both risk windows are indifferent.
The sign survey uses actual binary64 post-cost arithmetic without a neutral
tolerance band. It does not modify costs, source risks or the saved12decimal key.

This incentive appears frequently in recorded selected states, but does
**not** establish that it caused the20--26%funded drawdowns. It does not
reconstruct main holdings or execution, change discrete rankings, produce
alternative weights, attribute realized loss or validate a protective rule.
All financial rejections and the original15%DDgate remain.

| Predictor | Assessment benchmark | RMSE, bp/day | MAE, bp/day | Three-way direction match | MSE skill versus zero-active reference |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| unconditional_unit_ridge | SPY | 16.913889 | 12.388375 | 55.199115% | -0.293534% |
| unconditional_unit_ridge | QQQ | 20.124704 | 15.029162 | 56.637168% | +0.922430% |
| contextual_unit_ridge | SPY | 16.876732 | 12.281121 | 55.752212% | +0.146641% |
| contextual_unit_ridge | QQQ | 20.005767 | 14.867142 | 57.522124% | +2.090066% |

These are **forecast errors and descriptive error scores, not investment
returns**. The contextual predictor slightly lowers pooled errors, but some
individual sources remain worse than the zero-active reference. The reference
is not an implemented strategy, direction matches are not balanced-class
accuracy, and predicting negative assessment active rates need not create
profitable allocations. No significance, calibrated probability or future
financial qualification is established. All source-specific results remain in
the complete evidence rather than selecting favorable fragments.

Both learners use expanding completed-month observations and a fixed unit
coefficient prior, including the intercept. The context learner adds the two
known continuous126-session SPY/BIL and IEF/BIL relative-price returns.
At each date the preceding prediction is graded before learning that outcome,
then the next month is forecast. Initial partial October2016 is not learned;
the final incomplete October2026 is not graded. The seven unmatured decisions
retain null forecasts, not zero scores. All121dates,119learning months and
113graded months per source/benchmark/model remain. The904pooled expert-month
errors per table cell are correlated, not904independent months.

stress. This is not a robust qualified improvement.

falls0.230150/0.214120 points, stress Sharpe falls0.012842/0.010075 and
stress DD rises1.120775/1.096871 points. Annual turnover rises on every path,
by0.164366-0.375368x NAV. Ten-year tickets decline10 and fees decline;
five-year tickets rise3 and fees riseUSD13.69/26.07.
